受(1)年節效應、(2)持續控管低毛利銅材銷量，以及(3)印尼開採政
策等因素影響，華新 26Q1 營收下滑至新台幣 410.70 億元，季減
1.89%、年減 8.41%。營業利益達 5.47 億元，單季轉盈，但受整
體營收與毛利規模縮小影響 ， 仍年減42.40%。 但受惠業外收益35
億 挹 注 ， 稅 後 淨 利 成 長 至 35.85 億元， QoQ253.51% ，
YoY427.91%，單季 EPS 達 0.81 元。 
展望後市， 研究部認為除印泥政策對資源事業產生不確定性外 ， 其
餘核心事業營展望正向 ， 加上業外事業有望持續貢獻 ，預期全年將
呈現本業與合併獲利年成長態勢。研究部預期華新 2026 全年營收
為 1,807.08 億，YoY3.71%； 毛利率8.26%， 年增1.89 個百分點；
營業利益提升至 39.52 億；集團稅後獲利受惠業外挹注，提升至
166.36 億，YoY423.17%；預估 EPS 為 3.76 元；每股淨值約為
40.01 元。 
研究部依 2026 年底每股淨值 40.01 元為評價基礎，目前 PBR 約
為 0.83x，歷史評價區間為 0.5~1.3x。雖 2026 年整體營運改善，
且產業供需見好轉跡象，給予1xPBR，目標價 40 元，投資評等為
買進。  
 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
華新麗華核心業務主要從事電線電纜、鋼線鋼纜、特殊鋼、不
銹鋼及鎳生鐵的生產與銷售。除本業外，同時也跨足綠能光
電、商貿與地產等多元業務領域。公司的生產基地佈局全球，
涵蓋中國大陸多個城市，以及印尼、義大利等地。 
在營收結構上，不銹鋼事業為最大宗，佔比約五成；其次為佔
比近三成的電線電纜事業， 以及約佔兩成的資源事業(如鎳生
鐵、高冰鎳)；同時，公司積極佈局綠能轉型，投入離 岸風場
海纜市場，預計於 2027 年正式量產。在業外轉投資方面，華
新持有華邦電、華新科及華東等上市櫃公司股權。

⚫ 本業獲利改善疊加業外投資挹注，26Q1 EPS 為 0.81 元 
受(1)年節效應、(2)持續控管低毛利銅材銷量，以及(3)印尼開
採政策等因素影響，華新 26Q1 營收下滑至新台幣 410.70 億
元，季減 1.89%、年減 8.41%。 
各事業獲利方面： (1)不銹鋼受惠鎳與鎳生鐵價格同步上漲，
加上歐洲聖誕假期結束帶動需求 ， 在價量齊揚下毛利率明顯回
升；惟市場價格尚未完全回到貿易戰前水準，致使營收與毛利

⚫ 除權息狀況 率仍略低於 25Q1。(2)電線電纜事業銷量雖有季節性下滑，但
鑑於銅價上漲，公司持續控管低毛利之銅材銷售，雖使該部門
營收雖降，毛利率仍穩健維持於 12%。(3)資源事業則受印尼
政策影響，銷量與營收均呈年、季雙減；然因鎳生鐵售價逐步
反映鎳礦成本上揚，毛利率逆勢呈現年、季雙增。 
公司合併毛利率為 7.77%，季增 2.87 個百分點 ，年減0.47 個
百分點；營業利益達 5.47 億元，單季順利轉盈，但受整體營
收與毛利規模縮小影響，仍年減 42.40%。此外，業外收益為
35 億元，主要受惠於處分華邦電與華東科技等轉投資之股權
利益，以及權益法認列收益挹注。在業外大幅貢獻下，稅後淨
利成長至 35.85 億元，QoQ253.51%，YoY427.91%，單季
EPS 達 0.81 元。

⚫ 本業獲利提升，業外投資挹注，預估2026 年 EPS 為 3.76 元 
展望後市，研究部認為除印泥政策對資源事業產生不確定性
外，其餘核心事業營展望正向，加上業外事業有望持續貢獻，
預期全年將呈現本業與合併獲利年成長態勢。

不銹鋼：雖台灣市場下游需求尚未全面復甦， 但受惠原料報價
走揚及中國鋼鐵出口加持下，推升不鏽鋼產品報價，客戶預期
心理亦帶動接單成長；中國廠因反內捲政策限制供給，伴隨原
料漲價促使客戶回補庫存，預期呈現價量齊揚；歐洲廠則受惠
CBAM 與 Safegaurd 發酵，增加當地銷量，同時隨進入歐洲
傳統旺季，公司已自 3 月起逐步調漲當地報價，預期 2026 整
體不鏽鋼事業呈現價量齊揚態勢。

電線電纜：隨 Q1 傳統工作淡季結束，加上台電強韌電網計畫
及民間建廠的需求持續，預期26Q2 營收將穩步向上，但仍可
能因維穩獲利之銅材銷售策略壓抑營收增速。

資源事業：隨印尼鎳礦開採政策緊縮使鎳生鐵供給下滑，加上
印尼政府於 4 月實施鎳礦計價新制(HPM)， 推升鎳生鐵生產成
本，帶動成本與售價同步走升，預期後市毛利表現有望維持
26Q1 水準。

業外方面，受惠於記憶體供需帶動旗下轉投資事業獲利提升，
預估華新 2026 全年業外收益可達 159.35 億，貢獻EPS 表現

約為 40.01 元。

### Page 1

本刊載之報告為元富投顧於特定日期之分析，已力求陳述內容之可靠性，純屬研究性質，僅作參考，使用者應明瞭內容之時效性，
審慎考量投資風險，並就投資結果自行負責。報告著作權屬元富投顧所有，禁止任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。 
元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (115)金管投顧新字第 009 號 
公司拜訪快報

裕隆(2201) 
建議：維持買進 
結論：透過產線多樣化，爭取更多代工機會

一、透過產線多樣化，爭取更多代工機會 
台灣 2025 年新車銷售 41.4 萬輛，YoY -9%，主要為受
到進口關稅不確定壓抑需求，4Q25 在政府貨物稅優惠
政策延續下，銷售有回升。 
1~3 月新車銷售短期仍受關稅影響下滑 3%， 目前在貨物
稅政策延續與關稅問題影響放緩下，公司預期 2026 年
新車銷售較去年持平或小幅成長。而市場龍頭和泰車，
預期 2026 年新車銷售 44 萬輛，YoY 6.2%。 
裕隆透過產線多樣化與鴻華開發多款原型車，爭取更多
代工機會，目前 Foxtron Bria 與 MG 的 MPV 車代工已
順利量產。 
國內車市概況

二、納智捷售出，裕隆專注於生產代工 
鴻華先進以 7.876 億元收購裕隆子公司納智捷汽車全數
股權，取得納智捷的主導經營權。從「研發設計」領域
拓展至 「品牌與銷售」 ，的電動車一條龍服務。未來鴻華
先進除認列技術授權或銷售底盤的收入，將能認列整車
銷售的營收，擴大營運規模，不過初期因併購營運費用
增加，與未來銷售與品牌經營將更具挑戰。 
而對裕隆而言，納智捷的股權移轉案已於 2026 年 4 
月 10 日完成相關交割作業，自今年第二季起，納智捷
將不再列入裕隆的合併報表。以 2025 年數據推估，今
年將影響營收近 40 億元 。 不過納智捷過去的品牌營運與
行銷費用是不小的負擔，出售後，將可擺脫品牌經營風
險與財務壓力，公司專注於生產代工，有利母公司財務
改善。未來鴻華先進將以新品牌 Foxtron 推出新車款。
目前已推出 Model B 量產版的 Bria，第二款電動休旅
Cavira 將為繼 n7 後推出的車款，於 5/20 發表並開始預
接訂單，將於 6/17 正式上市。 
此外，鴻華先進 2Q25 與三菱簽署合作備忘錄，開發右
駕電動車，布局海外市場，預計 2026 年下半年進軍紐
澳市場，並委由裕隆代工於台灣生產，也將挹注未來成
長動能。 
三、預期 2026 年 EPS 1.5 元 
裕隆 1Q26 營收 169.75 億元 ，-8.3%QoQ，-3.5%YoY，
主要為整體車市仍在調整及金融服務收益減少。毛利率
與營業利益受惠成本結構優化以及費用控制得宜，優於
預期，不過業外受轉投資公司仍在調整影響，單季 EPS 
0.32 元與預估相當。 
展望 2026 年，鴻華先進推出款兩新車款，目前集團旗
下裕日車轉投資東風日產，與中華車陸續引進新車型，
有助於挹注營運動能，加上政府推出貨物稅減徵，預期
2026 年營收 704.44 億元 ，YoY -2.6%。自 2Q26 納智捷
品牌出售後，未來專注代工，將有利毛利率轉好，推估
EPS 1.5 元。 
四、 「整車製造」及「能源發展」雙核心方向發展  
公司除了持續拓展汽車代工業務，並啟動動力電池量產
製造。集團擁有研發工業型儲能機櫃及電池管理系統能
力，推動儲能產品，提升能源生產規模。未來朝「整車
製造」及「能源發展」雙核心方向發展。 
新店裕隆城其主體建物樓層為 B3~8F，約4.3 萬坪 ，B1F
～4F 為誠品商場 、5F~6F 為商場 、7F～8F 為威秀影廳。
目前來店人次穩定增加，每年可貢獻組金收入 5 億元。 
五、維持買進理由 
(1)新品牌新車款挹注成長 
(2)專注代工

1 
2436 偉詮電 
(Weltrend Semiconductor Co.)

報告日期： 2026/05/26 研 究 員：方姿婷  (Lesley Fang) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 偉詮電為電源管理與類比 IC 廠，電源、智慧應用  
偉詮電(2436)是發展混合類比、數位線路 IC 設計廠商。偉詮電產
品組合為，1Q26 電源管理 IC 與類比 IC 佔 26%、智慧應用 IC 佔 
43%、電子元件代銷佔 31%，其中智慧應用 IC 成長迅速。產品涵
蓋 MCU、各式數位與類比 IC、擴充至影像處理、影像辨識、高效
能直流無刷馬達等各式演算法以及 8b/32b 泛用型 MCU 等 ， 也代理
ROHM Semi 等品牌之電子零組件。

二. 2026 年 PC/NB 量衰退，在電競、商用與遊戲機進展 
以偉詮電 2025 年產品線 發展，在電源管理 IC 與類比 IC，應用佔
比最高區塊為 NB、遊戲機，NB 市場於 2025 年在庫存方面已漸漸
去化，預期在 Type-C 滲透率提升之下，將有一部分庫存回補之急
單效應。若以 2024~2025 年 Type-C/PD 之市場規模看法，偉詮電在
NB USB Type-C/USB PD 領域於 2024 年滲透率已高達 70%，2025
年滲透率更會達到 80-90%，NB 量增以 AI PC/NB 規格提升之下對
於 USB Type-C/PD 等需求也會提升。偉詮電本身優勢在於電源相
關、DDIC、類比 IC，結合高壓製程、MCU、CPU、韌體製造之整
合能力，在 NB 市場市佔率高於 70%，且在 USB PD、USB 3.1 規格
升級之下受惠。即使 2 0 2 6 年 P C / N B 出貨量預估衰退雙位數
-10%YoY，但偉詮電預估 USB PD 與 Type-C 在電競筆電、商用機
種、遊戲機等新品推展之下 2026 年可望持平或小幅增長。

三.  陞達科技風扇與散熱朝伺服器發展，成為關注焦點 
伺服器散熱風扇的產品線持續整合，偉詮電主要做 MCU，陞達科
主要做 ASIC，公司認為 MCU 的需求會越來越明顯，產品分高階
到低階都會做到 。 伺服器的風扇 ， 主要出貨以 12V 為主 ， 未來 1H26
會發展 48V 產品。USB PD 3.0 過渡到 3.1 或是散熱風扇 IC 由 12V
過渡到 48V ，ASP 可以提高近 2 倍，伺服器散熱風扇的產品發展進
程，成為近期市場關注焦點。NVDA 之液冷 GB200 採用的是 Open 
Loop Liquid Cooling 相當於液體、加上 10 顆~20 顆風扇氣冷(偉詮
電於液冷水泵馬達控制 I C、液冷風扇馬達控制 I C 已經進入
Design-in 階段) ， 公司認為氣冷或 Hybrid 方式還是會是主流 ， 因此 ，
對於散熱風扇控制 IC 市場用於 AI 伺服器的成長看法相對樂觀。
投資建議：區間操作→買進 
目標價位：100.00 元

2 
展望 2026 年，NVDA 伺服器搭載新設計，目前 AI 伺服器用散熱
風扇，一台平均搭載 12 顆以上，NVDA 設計為一整機櫃、機櫃旁
SideCar、PowerRack 獨立出來，以 NVDA GB 300 為例，散熱風扇
需要 48V 或 54V 驅動器加上 BBU6L 之後共 40~50 顆風扇(GB 系列
合計需 350 顆)。NVDA Rubin 系列，也採用 54V 驅動器搭載 1~2
座 PowerRack，一座 Rack 需要 70~80 顆散熱風扇(傳統 AC 與 EC 
BLDC 合計) 。偉詮電提及，自身於 AI 伺服器散熱風扇，市佔率至
少 30~50%之間，4Q25 已有一個美系知名 CSP 客戶積極拉貨，未
來 AI 伺服器用散熱風扇會再進軍 ASIC 市場成為下一營運動能。
由於伺服器用散熱風扇所需功能涵蓋逆風齊轉、斷電煞車、電源
與溫度偵測等多項整合型功能，偉詮電在於風扇設計之整合性解
決方案，能夠提供給 CSP 客戶更為完整且性價比高的方案。

四.投資建議：買進，目標價 100 元 
偉詮電之產品 AC/DC Switching Regulators、PWM Controllers、USB 
PD Controllers、Fast Charge Prorocol Controllers 等涵蓋 MCU、各式
數位與類比 IC、擴充至影像處理、影像辨識、高效能直流無刷馬
達等各式演算法以及 8b/32b 泛用型 MCU 等，也代理電子零組件。
此外，人型機器人，也需要馬達控制 IC，一人型機器人或倉儲機
器狗需要 20-30 顆馬達控制 IC，機器人關節需 60V 控制 IC 之操作
電壓，屬於高單價產品，目前偉詮電已跟知名學府合作倉儲機器
狗用於智慧工廠運作，機器人的馬達控制 IC 會是下一個成長動
能。展望 2026 年，預估 偉詮電營收為 43.76 億元，+22.61%YoY，
毛利率為 35.93%，稅後淨利為 9.20 億元，稅後 EPS+4.61 元。給予
偉詮電投資建議由區間操作調整至買進，目標價 100 元(22.00x 
2026EPS)。

營收走勢圖

研究員/吳大任 TERRY.WU@uni-psg.com

研究部維持對偉詮電的買進投資建議，係因(1)1Q26 本業獲利大
幅提升且優於預期，(2)2Q26 具有上修動能，(3)雖然 AI SERVER
走向液冷，但新增 POWER RACK 風扇需求，且 FAN DRIVER 單價將
提升，(4)上修 26 年營收及獲利預估。綜合以上，預估偉詮電26
年 EPS 達到 4.51 元(原估 3.89 元，+64%YOY)，雖然1Q26 因為業
外獲利較少使 EPS 不如預期，不過本業成長動能躍升，證明其產
品競爭力及技術，故給予買進建議，評價給予26 年的 20 倍本益
比，目標價 90 元。

重點摘要：

1. 1Q26 本業獲利優於預期： 
偉詮電 1Q26 營收為 創 歷 史新 高的 10.47 億元，成 長
13.2%QOQ，成長 31.7%YOY，其中主要是 SERVER 風扇馬達
驅動 IC 出貨持續增長 ， 使得1Q26 智慧應用佔比從 4Q25 的
36%提升至 1Q26 的 43%， 而此產線毛利率較高 ， 也使得1Q26
偉詮電毛利率提升至 33.5%， 優於原先預期的32.3%， 整體
營業利益跳升至 1.42 億元，大幅優於先前預估的 8200 萬
元，OPM 也跳升至 13.6%。業外利益僅有 3100 萬元，相對
於 4Q25 大幅降低 ， 係因大盤在三月底較動盪 ， 轉投資評價
利益較少 。 使得稅後淨利僅為1.38 億元(原估 1.92 億元)，
衰退 53.3%QOQ，成長 466.6%YOY，EPS 0.7 元(原估 0.96
元)， 但就營業利益來看則是大幅優於原先預期約35-40%。

2. 2Q26 具上修動能： 
偉詮電已經公告 4 月營收為 3.9 億元，創歷史同期新高，
主要仍是 SERVER 風扇相關 IC 拉貨動能受到 AI SERVER 需
求帶動 ， 需求增長幅度持續擴大 ， 故研究部上修2Q26 營收
預估為 11.93 億元(原估 10.78 億元)，成長14%QQO，成長
28.6%YOY，另外因為智慧相關應用佔比持續提升，推估毛
利率將提升至 34.4%(原估 33.1%)，稅後淨利預估同步上
修 2.72 億元(原估 2.15 億元)，成長 98.5%QOQ，成長
988%YOY，EPS 1.36 元(原估 1.07 元)。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. 走向液冷，但風扇在散熱產值中仍持續增長： 
公司在法說中表示 ， 雖然目前AI SERVER散熱走向液冷 ， 但
多數仍是L2A， 故風扇整櫃用量約是350個 ， 進入到VR系列 ，
會開始走向L2L規格，單櫃風扇會減少，但是會出現POWER 
RACK，一櫃COMPUTE RACK搭配2櫃的POWER RACK，而一櫃的
POWER RACK將會使用72個風扇 ， 另外因為走48V， 相對於先
前12V，單顆FAN IC 將會是先前的一倍以上，故走向VR系
列 ， 就算L2L都未用風扇貨或是水泵下 ， 整體FAN IC產值將
不會減少。先前認為走向VR系列，將會走向液冷，故下半
年FAN IC成長幅度將趨緩，但因為FAN IC單價將提升，且
有POWER RACK風扇挹注下，上修26年智慧相關IC產線營收
成長70-80%YOY，原估50%YOY。

4. 產品組合優化狀況優於預期，上修毛利率預估 
偉詮電主要有三大產品，分別為 POWER 相關(USB PD 及
SWITHCING)、智慧應用(FAN DRIVER)及代理羅姆半導體產
品(分離式元件)，毛利率區間分別為，POWER約略低於平
均，FAN DRIVER高於平均，而代理產品毛利率約11-13%。
因為FAN DRIVER需求優於預期，成長性比原先預估更高，
加上羅姆半導體的分離式元件有所漲價，故將抵銷晶圓代
工及封測的漲價，進而帶動毛利率的增長，上修26年毛利
率預估至34.7%(原估33.3%)。

5. 本業動能優於預期，氣冷散熱有延續性 
1Q26偉詮電受惠到客戶對FAN DRIVER拉貨動能強勁，使得
本業獲利優於預期，雖然業外獲利不如前季，但從本業可
以看出產品組合優化對於營業利益的大幅帶動。展望未
來，2Q26將持續上修，且雖然VR系列走向液冷，但在出現
POWER RACK風冷需求，加上FAN DRIVER因為電壓值提高而
使單價提升，相對先前對於風冷較為悲觀的想法有所扭
轉，故上修26年偉詮電營收為46.5億元(原估42.3億元)，
成長30.3%YOY ，稅後淨利預估為 9.01 億元(原估7.78億
元)，成長64%YOY，EPS 4.51元(原估3.89元)。

Data Centers: Asia Communacopia + Technology — Key Takeaways: AI
Infrastructure: Powering the Next Intelligence Cycle
19 May 2026 | 8:26PM HKT
Presenters: Mark Fong, CEO (Empyrion Digital), Yaniv Ghitis (Chief Commercial and
Investment Offic er, Digital Edge), Stephen Watts (CEO, PaleBlueDot AI)
Bottom Line: 1) Power as the key bottleneck affec ting construction timelines and

delivery capability, with public-private partnership on grid infrastructure builds
needed to bridge the gap. 2) AI compresses timelines, with single-tenant GPU
customers signing contracts in 6 weeks vs. 4–6 months for traditional hyperscalers.
3) Data center operators preserving fungibility to cater toward both traditional
and AI-firs t customers, e.g. hybrid air/liquid cooling, variable rack densities, and
delaying data hall fit-out to the last possible moment. 4) Supply would sell out

within 3–6 months at current demand levels if it existed, but expansion is
constrained by power, permitting, water, and increasingly community acceptance. 5)
Geopolitics raises compliance overhead but has not dampened underlying demand.
Japan, India, the Philippines flagg ed as key upside growth markets.
Key Takeaways
Power as the key bottleneck
While GPU shortages should normalize by 2028–2029; power is the keyn
constraint affecting construction timelines and delivery capability, because

resolving grid infrastructure issues requires 5 to 10 years.
Public-private partnership is the key to answer who bears responsibility forn
infrastructure builds: Governments should take the lead given power’s nature
as a national utility, with private operators and hyperscalers increasingly
investing directly in power generation and renewables to bridge the gap while
contributing through utility tariffs. Malaysia was highlighted as a market where

government-utility collaboration is working effectively.
Power efficiency (PUE) vs water tradeoff: Differ ent markets demand tailoredn
approaches based on local resource scarcity and pricing. Digital Edge highlighted

### Page 2

its Mumbai facility as a case study where it is sourcing treated wastewater from a
nearby purifica tion plant rather than potable water. The site features the largest
water-based chiller plant in its class and delivers PUE below 1.25.
Empyrion foresees that the four pillars of infrastructure builds (power, water, n
connectivity, permits) are being joined by a fifth: community acceptance.
Operators who successfully engage local communities will gain a competitive edge.

How shifting customer mix is affec ting data center operators
Single-tenant AI customers vs. traditional multi-tenant — differ ent lead times:n
Empyrion noted that single-tenant GPU customers show commitment on orders 15–
24 months before construction starts, locking in bespoke designs early. Traditional
multi-tenant customers, by contrast, expect only 3–6 months of lead time and more
standardized infrastructure. Empyrion aims to delay data hall fit-out to the last

possible moment to cater to both groups.
Hyperscalers vs. neoclouds — radically different commercial speed: New AI-firs tn
customers and neoclouds are pushing for modular and prefab designs to compress
delivery timelines. They also move faster commercially, with Empyrion noting new
contracts can close in 6 weeks vs. 4–6 months with hyperscalers. As a GPUaaS
provider who transforms energy into tokens, PaleBlueDot cited a Japan project that
delivered 6,000 GPUs in 89 days. Speed to fulfill the demand has become a key

differentiator from a customer perspective.
Operator response — modularity, fungibility: The panel agreed that hybridn
air/liquid cooling is now the default design choice to support both GPU-intensive AI
and traditional cloud workloads in the same facility. Digital Edge engineers its
facilities to support rack densities from 20kW to over 1MW — a wide envelope that
avoids over-optimizing for AI at the expense of other workload types.
Growth outlook

Biggest upside markets: India is expected to grow rapidly, supported by favorablen
demographics, deep engineering talent, and proximity to the Middle East. Digital
Edge is already executing on this thesis via its 300MW Navi Mumbai campus. Japan
was also flagg ed as a standout with government-backed initiatives driving aggressive
expansion; alongside the Philippines with reduced red tape and scalable power.
Training vs inference: PaleBlueDot noted that c.70% of its AI compute demand isn

now inference. Company expects the demand to continue to scale as
application-layer consumption has yet to be fully realized.
Boldest predictions and forward-looking themes: Empyrion framed the AI-drivenn
demand surge as non-zero-sum where traditional cloud workloads continue to
grow alongside new GPU and AI-first demand, expanding total market size rather
than redistributing it. On the supply side, consolidation among DC operators is
expected to improve efficiency and reduce power/chip constraints.

Geopolitics
Regulatory shifts have caused operational adjustments (e.g. enhanced duen

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y
ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Medium-term 2026/27 guidance
• Revenue: +30-35% y/y (implying the high end of the communicated €2.1-
2.4bn range)
• EBITDA margin: 25-29% (raised from 24-28% previously)
• *This guidance is subject to supply and the geopolitical situation, as well as
currency developments. Also not included are the potential effects of

ongoing negotiations with customers. These would result in a capacity
expansion that is partially financed by customers and would accordingly
impact earnings performance and capital expenditure planning.
Other key takeaways:
• (+) FY26/27 revenue growth will be mainly driven by volume growth, with
Microelectronics (ABF substrate) seeing more pronounced growth from AI
infra investments, which impact demand for both GPU and increasingly CPU
as the ecosystem broadens towards more inference workloads.

• (+) AT&S noted that customer demand for its technologies is so large that
some of them cannot be fulfilled; particularly for Microelectronics, it is
seeing strong demand from both existing and new customers.
• (+) Now AT&S' agreements with its customers often include financing for
investments from its customers, one example being the newly announced
capacity expansion for high-end substrates in Chongqing, China, for which the
required investments in the 'high double-digit € million' range will be fully

financed based on long-term customer agreements. This is expected to
ensure profitability for AT&S.
• (+) AT&S also noted increasing input costs from suppliers, which AT&S
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
AT&S (ATSV.VI, not covered) reported C1Q revenue of €477mn (+2% q/q):
Electronics Solutions (-13% q/q) and Microelectronics (ABF; +14% q/q).
C1Q/F4Q Microelectronics (ABF) EBIT margin jumped by 920bp q/q to 17.4%,
which management attributed to bigger volumes in all sites and pricing effects.
FY26/27 revenue was guided to grow 30-35% y/y, primarily driven by volumes,

with Microelectronics (ABF) seeing more pronounced growth...
...and EBITDA margin was guided to fall in the range of 25-29% (vs. 23.3% in
FY25/26), on the back of volumes, pricing and passing on increasing input costs.
May 21, 2026 04:11 PM GMT

### Page 2

M
Update
2
expects to pass on to its customers, though typically with a delay of a few
months. However, management is confident that it will be able to balance
cost increases and price hikes for the remainder of this year.
• (+) AT&S confirmed that it did adjust the pricing for its ABF substrate
products in C1Q/F4Q.
• (=)  AT&S is also engaging with partners and customers to develop future
technologies, including larger substrates, glass core substrates, embedded

component substrates/PCBs, optical PCBs, etc.
Our thoughts: Management sounded very positive during the call, citing several
growth tailwinds for ABF substrates, including larger volumes, more customer
commitments and continuing pricing actions to offset inflationary cost pressures.
This echoes our view that we are just in the early phase of an upcycle, and that the
substrate suppliers will be able to expand margins through pricing adjustments that

more than offset the cost pressures. We believe AT&S' view on ABF substrates
aligns with our positive view on Unimicron, NYPCB, and Zhen Ding.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 15%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.

Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers
n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn&#39;t require substrate
(e.g., CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Zhen Ding (4958.TW)

Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 10%, a medium-
term growth rate of 15% and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Higher-than-expected F-PCB content increase for iPhones
n Better-than-expected production yield/share allocation for SLP
n Share gains on higher margin F-PCB pieces
Risks to Downside
n Worse-than-expected iPhone sell-through
n Lower-than-expected F-PCB content increase for iPhones

n Worse-than-expected production yield/share allocation for SLP
n Increasing competition from Chinese peers, intensifying pricing pressure

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

updates. We see stronger TSMC CoWoS expansion. We remain

positive on the NVDA supply chain, and raise our PT for AllRing.
Computex runs June 2–5, with the NVIDIA CEO keynote set for June 1. We expect
demos of AI server and networking system designs, not limited to NVIDIA. Key focus:
1. Rubin series sever rack designs: We look forward to the combination of
NVL (GPU rack Kyber with CPO scale-up connection), LPU rack, Vera CPU
rack, BlueField rack, and NVSwitch rack - together targeting the lowest cost
per token for a future AI Factory. We will see if AMD demonstrates Helios

racks sharing token output data.
2. The US$20bn NVIDIA CPU opportunity: Our US semi analyst Joe Moore
expects NVIDIA to provide more details. Our supply chain checks suggest
TSMC is allocating additional CoWoS-R capacity and 3nm wafers for the
Vera CPU. Based on capacity, we think 1.5mn units of standalone CPUs is a
reasonable assumption. End customers could be Microsoft, Meta,
CoreWeave, and Oracle.
3. What about the Rubin schedule and Rubin Ultra package design?

Investors wonder whether Rubin GPU can achieve its target TDP of 2.3kW.
There is also some doubt that Rubin Ultra will be 4-die per package, as
warpage issue still encountered. Our understanding is that Rubin was rolled
back to the original chip thermal solution and achieved 1.8kW TDP; through
server-system thermal collaboration, the 2.3kW TDP can still be achieved.
For Rubin Ultra, NVIDIA needs new photomasks to adopt HBM4e in 2027,
and it should stick with 2-die per package to maintain good chip yield.

We lift our TSMC 2027 CoWoS capacity assumption to 200kwpm from
170kwpm, and slightly trim our SoIC capacity build forecasts: Very strong AI GPU
and CPU demand led TSMC to further expand CoWoS capacity in AP7.  Our checks
suggest TSMC is turning its Fab 15A 28nm/22nm space to 55nm interposer
production. Hence UMC should receive order overflow from Sony’s 28/22nm ISP in
2027; we remain OW on UMC. For SoIC, we still see production unchanged at
30/60kwpm in 2027/2028, while production build might be slightly delayed for

three quarters; we therefore trim our capacity build assumptions to 40/70kwpm for
2027/2028, from 45/78kwpm, respectively. We remain OW on NVIDIA for its key
supply chain for Rubin and Vera, including KYEC, ASE, Hon Precision and  Winway, let
alone TSMC in foundry. We lift AllRing’s price target to NT$1,580, from NT$1,280,
reflecting strong CoWoS capacity build, especially in 2027.

Global CoWoS Capacity Update
CoWoS capacity – update on capacity build
Driven by very strong demand coming from AI GPU and CPU and potential rising
competition from other 2.5D packaging solutions, TSMC has decided to further expand its
CoWoS capacity  in AP7 at the expense of SoIC’s capacity expansion, which is less urgent.
We revise our 2027 CoWoS capacity estimate from 160-170kwpm to 200kwpm. TSMC has
confirmed that it is turning its Fab 15A 28nm/22nm space to 55nm interposer production

by adding around 10kwpm, which is further new expansion this year after just building
15kwpm of interposer capacity in Singapore and filling all interposer capacity in Fab 12 and
Fab 14. Hence, we think UMC could receive the order overflow from Sony’s 28nm/22nm
ISP in 2027, and we reiterate OW on UMC.
For SoIC, we still expect production of 30kwpm for 2027 and 60kwpm for 2028. The
production build might be slightly delayed for 3 quarters, and hence we trim our capacity

build assumptions from  45kwpm in 2027 and 78kwpm in 2028 to 40kwpm and 70kwpm,
respectively.

SoIC capacity and demand breakdown
Current customers adopting SoIC include:
• NVIDIA (covered by Joseph Moore): We expect annual SoIC consumption of 6k in
2026, rising to 120k in 2027. This includes demand for CPO's COUPE. We see more
clues from the Asian supply chain that NVIDIA's next-generation GPU Feynman
could require 1-2GB of SRAM stacked on each die within the Feynman package. On
the other hand, we also see NVIDIA studying GPU-to-GPU stacking, which should

translate to SoIC demand.
• AMD (covered by Joseph Moore): AMD has adopted SoIC on its AI and gaming
GPU and high-end CPU, and we  do not rule out future adoption for its ASIC
business. We  expect AMD's SoIC consumption at 42k in 2026, increasing to 60k in
2027.
• Apple (covered by Erik Woodring): Apple's data center and Mac Pro chips rely on
SoIC. We expect Apple's SoIC consumption at 36k in 2026, increasing to 60k in
2027.
• Others: Several customers are now trying TSMC's SoIC capacity, including

Broadcom, Qualcomm (both covered by Joseph Moore), and AWS. We think this is
in line with our understanding that Broadcom would start to introduce SoIC
packaging for its networking chips and ASIC solutions. However, some appear to
still be in the design and trial stage, and not yet finalized.

AllRing: Bottom-up Analysis
We expect CoWoS-related revenue to account for 75% of AllRing's total revenue in 2026,
with AllRing focusing on oS equipment supply to TSMC and ASE/SPIL. However, we see
more non-TSMC 2.5 packaging solution vendors qualifying more Taiwanese CoWoS
equipment vendors, creating a new TAM for AllRing, including FoPLP and Intel's EMIB. We
expect AllRing's 2027 CoWoS revenue to rise 62% Y/Y (TSMC's CoWoS seen at 200kwpm

for 2027), and see it beginning to prepare equipment for CoPoS (if the trial round goes
smoothly in 2026) and its AP fab in Arizona (MP likely starting in 2028).
For CPO, AllRing has penetrated the CPO supply chain of NVIDIA for FAU-optical engine
coupling and FAU AOI equipment. Our latest supply chain checks suggest that AllRing may
have also won the dispensing step after insertion 3, where the optical engine will be
mounted onto the CPO package substrate. We expect more clarity from the company on

CPO-related revenue in 2H26, and expect it to account for 13%, 21%, and 28% of AllRing's
total revenue in 2026, 2027, and 2028, respectively.
For SoIC, we expect 2026 SoIC capacity to reach 14kwpm. TSMC further expanding its
CoWoS capacity in AP7 could cause slightly delays in equipment pull-in. Our latest supply
chain checks suggest that TSMC has revised 2027e capacity to 40kwpm (from 45kwpm)
and 2028e to 70kwpm (from 75kwpm). AllRing is the sole WoW dispenser supplier for

SoIC, but we expect the delay in SoIC to be more than offset by the strong build in
CoWoS. In the long run,  we still  expect it to be the key growth driver for AllRing. SoIC is
one of the key advanced packaging nodes for TSMC in the coming years, as we see more
and more customers adopting this technology to realize chiplet design, including AMD,
NVIDIA, Apple, and Broadcom.

packaging opportunities from the non-TSMC camp.

AllRing: Valuation Methodology
We lift our RI-based price target (base case scenario value) from NT$1,280 to NT
$1,580: We factor in our estimate changes across 2026-28.  Our other key assumptions are
unchanged, including a cost of equity (CoE) at 8%, derived from a risk-free rate of 2.0%,
beta of 1.0 and market risk premium of 6%, intermediate growth rate of 16% and terminal
growth rate of 4.5%. Our bull and bear case values also rise, from NT$1,800 and NT$520

to NT$2,200 and NT$640, respectively, implying 56x and 16x our 2027 EPS estimate.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

徐銘晙 
Michael.MC.Hsu@Yuanta.com 
李秉睿 
pinjui.lee@yuanta.com

1Q26 EPS 符合預期，主因 AI GPU/手機相關需求成長 
1Q26 毛利率 43%，略優於預期 0.5ppts 主因 MEMS 探針卡進入大量採購階
段成本結構逐漸改善。本業獲利季增 64%至 77.7 億元，符合預期，主因產
能建置費用提升抵銷毛利成長，EPS 達 19.5 元，符合預期。預估 2Q26 營
收季增 16%，GM 將季增 0.4ppts 至 43.4%，EPS 22.6 元，主因 GPU/手機
測試座及 HPC 探針卡持續拉貨。 
公司新產能提前開出，以滿足 CPU/GPU/ASIC 測試座需求 
公司預期在擴增既有廠房產能以及新廠仁武一廠貢獻下， 2026 年底測試座
產能將年增 110%至每月 4.2K pin，1H27 產能將在仁武二廠提前一年投入
量產下，成長至每月 7.2K pin。2028 年則是在 1Q28 另一新大樓導入量產下
使產能持續倍增。展望 2026 年成長動能將來自 1) 兩大客戶 AI GPU 
FT/SLT 需求持續強勁、2) GPU/ASIC 客戶 CPU 受惠代理 AI 需求開始進入
35%以上 TAM CAGR 的高速成長期、3) MEMS 探針卡需求穩健，帶動營收
年增 76%，GM 年減 1.3ppts 至 44%，EPS 達 102.6 元。預估 2027/28 年
除了 AI 加速器出貨量提升外，在大尺寸 GPU/ASIC/CPO 晶片占比提升，高
單價 Hypersocket 在大尺寸測試的優勢將成為提升 SLT 產品市佔率的關鍵。
帶動 2027 年營收年增 94%，GM 年增 3.2ppts 達到 47.2%，EPS 達 212 元。 
測試座供需同步成長趨勢明確，目標價 11,000 元 
在先進製程/封裝產能持續開出、台積電於技術論壇重申晶片尺寸成長趨勢、
晶片維持高速迭代之下，測試總量將持續提升。預期 公司憑藉優異的售後服
務能力以及充足的產能將維持既有市場外， Hypersocket 優勢亦將在 2027
年後晶片進入大尺寸世代顯現，帶動 2025-2028 年獲利將以 92% CAGR 成
長，預估 2028 年 EPS 328 元，因此基於 2027 年的 213 元 EPS 搭配優於
國內外同業的 52 倍本益比，給予買進評等，目標價 11000 元。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
1Q26 EPS 符合預期，主因新一代 AI GPU/手機測試座拉貨需求強勁，
帶動 AI/HPC 及手機業務年增 80%/85%。 
⧫  
受惠於 1) CPU TAM 將以 35% 以上 CAGR 成長至 2030 年；2) 測試
時間提升趨勢不變； 3) 兩大 AI 客戶 FT/SLT 需求成長 ，預計 公司
2027 測試座總產能將較 2026 成長 80%，並在 1Q28 持續增加。 
⧫  
受惠兩大 AI 客戶 CPU/GPU 測試座需求成長明確且大尺寸晶片趨勢帶
動高單價新品滲透率提升下，預計 2025-2028 年獲利 CAGR 將達
90%，給予目標價 11,000 元，係基於 52x 2027 年 EPS 213 元。

抵銷毛利成長，EPS 達 19.5 元，符合預期。

2Q26 營收預期季增 16%，主因 GPU/HPC 探針卡持續成長 
預估在 AI GPU/CPU 持續帶動 HPC socket 及 MEMS 探針卡出貨成長之下，2Q26 營收將季增
16%，GM 也將在 MEMS 探針合約進入量產階段，將季增 0.4 ppts 至 43.4%，營業費用在產能提
前建置而維持高檔下，季持平，使本業獲利率季增 2.6 ppt/年減 1.5 ppt 至 28.7%，EPS 季增 16% 
達 22.6 元。

公司新產能提前開出，以滿足 CPU/GPU/ASIC 測試座需求 
公司預期在擴增既有廠房產能以及新廠仁武一廠貢獻下， 2026 年底測試座產能將年增 110%至每
月 4.2K pin，1H27 產能將在仁武二廠提前一年投入量產下，成長至每月 7.2K p in。2028 年則是在
1Q28 另一新大樓導入量產下使產能持續倍增。展望 2026 年成長動能將來自 1) 兩大客戶 AI GPU 
FT/SLT 需求持續強勁、2) GPU/ASIC 客戶 CPU 受惠代理 AI 需求開始進入 35%以上 TAM CAGR
的高速成長期、3) MEMS 探針卡需求穩健，帶動營收年增 76%，GM 年減 1.3ppts 至 44%，EPS
達 102.6 元 。 預 估 2027/28 年 除 了 既 有 客 戶 AI 加速器產品出貨量提升外，在大尺寸
GPU/ASIC/CPO 晶片占比提升，高單價 Hypersocket 在大尺寸測試的優勢將成為提升 SLT 產品市
佔率的關鍵。帶動 2027 年營收年增 94%，GM 年增 3.2ppts 達到 47.2%，EPS 達 212 元。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體 2026 年 5 月 27 日 WinWay 第4 頁，共 16頁  
產業概況  
測試產業在 AI 晶片出貨比重提升下，地位越趨重要 
測試在 IC 製造過程中是一個關鍵的過程，而其角色的重要性正不斷增加。主因是製程節點的
微縮和先進封裝的進步，使得 IC 結構更為複雜，導致製程中的良率降低。因此，為了維持良
率並縮短產品上市時間，測試在產業中的地位越趨重要。

圖 4：提升晶片良最終率對成本下降效益最大 圖 5：靜態資料毀損(Silent Data Corruption)

資料來源：IEEE、元大投顧預估 資料來源：META、元大投顧預估

其中靜態資料毀損(Silent Data Corruption)在晶片失效中最難以解決的問題，主因 SDC 發生時
系統不會偵測到錯誤訊號，讓使用者可能得到錯誤的運算結果或進行資料搜索結果，此問題在
必須花費大量成本與時間的模型訓練尤為嚴重。靜態資料毀損 SDC 是由晶片內微小製程缺陷
造成，此缺陷是僅在運用到晶片特定結構及特定工作環境下才會出現的隱性錯誤。現行測試
（wafer test、final test、Burn-in、SLT）雖然測試覆蓋率已高於過往的晶片，但仍缺乏針對的
特定條件的測試覆蓋率。由於晶片於資料中心發生失效的成本在目前高單價晶片以及大叢集的
資料中心架構下，將遠高於增加測試覆蓋率以及提升測試流程技術的資本支出。目前半導體產
業正積極增加對關鍵層（ contact/via/M1–M2）的壓力測試以及整體測試流程的覆蓋率來提高
良率。此外在晶片設計階段優化 DFT 的設計並在晶片部屬於資料中心時增加更多內建監測及
週期性測試亦是產業趨勢之一。因此本中心預期測試產業在晶片產出數量以及複雜度持續提升
下將進入高速成長週期。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體 2026 年 5 月 27 日 WinWay 第5 頁，共 16頁  
圖 6：半導體測試流程主要分為前端 (CP) 和後端 (FT)

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧預估

⚫ 晶片測試 (CP): CP 的主要目的是在晶圓被切割成晶片並進入封裝過程之前，檢測缺陷晶
片，從而節省缺陷晶片的冗餘封裝成本，同時監控代工良率。 CP 測試項目通常包括直
流(DC)測試，該測試評估半導體裝置的基本電氣特性，例如電壓、電流和電阻。  
⚫ 最終測試 (FT): 除了 CP 中相同的 DC 測試外，FT 還有更多測試類別，例如晶粒的功能
和交流(AC)測試，以測試晶粒的邏輯和時間特性。雖然 FT 與 CP 的部分測試項目有重疊，
但採用先進節點和封裝技術製造的 IC 在精確控制的測試環境下承擔了更嚴格的測試輸出
分析。相較之下，成熟節點 IC 的測試流程通常會更加簡潔，以有效的降低測試成本。 
⚫ 預機測試(Burn-in)：由於需要在老化物理特性消失前的 3 天內進行測試，因此老化測試
通常會合併到 FT 測試。老化測試時，根據客戶測試要求，晶片被送入老化爐，承受不
同溫度範圍(-40℃~150℃)和功率(<1 瓦-1500 瓦)的測試。過往，僅有在極端環境或壽命
較短的晶片會進行老化測試，而如今，因為 AI/HPC 晶片需要在高熱、高功率環境下持
續工作，讓老化測試再次成為投資者關注的焦點。 
⚫ 系統級測試 (SLT): 有別於 CP 與 FT，SLT 測試可透過非同步處理器(asynchronous 分
選機)進行高平行測試，更著重於檢驗晶片在最終使用時的功能。SLT 將 DUT 連接到不
同測試站的系統層級測試板上，重現不同的終端使用者環境，評估裸晶在指定軟體中操
作時的功能性與相容性，有助大幅縮短最新產品的上市時間。

獲利調整與股票評價  
測試座供需同步成長趨勢明確，目標價 11,000 元 
在先進製程/封裝產能持續開出、台積電於技術論壇重申晶片尺寸成長趨勢、晶片維持高速迭代之
下，測試總量將持續提升。預期 公司憑藉優異的售後服務能力以及充足的產能將維持既有市場外，
Hypersocket 優勢亦將在 2027 年後晶片進入大尺寸世代顯現，此外 MEMS 探針卡亦將成為公司另
一穩定成長動能，帶動 2025-2028 年獲利以 92% CAGR 成長，預估 2028 年 EPS 328 元，因此基
於 2027 年的 213 元 EPS 搭配優於國內外同業的 52 倍本益比，給予買進評等，目標價 11000 元。

公司簡介  
穎崴成立於 2001 年，為國內 IC 測試介面製造商，主要提供半導體測試治具(Test Socket) 及探針卡(Probe Card) 之設計、
開發 與生 產製 造 ，範 圍涵 蓋晶 圓測 試(CP) 、 最終 測 試(FT)、系 統測 試(SLT) 、 老化 測試(Burn-In)及 溫控 系統(Thermal 
Control System) 。其主要客戶包含 AMD 與 NVIDIA 等國際 IC 設計大廠。國外同業包含 Cohu、Enplas 與 Yokowo 等，國
內則包含精測、旺矽、雍智科技等廠商。

### Page 13

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體 2026 年 5 月 27 日 WinWay 第13 頁，共 16頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 穎崴整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於領先的位置，
且在半導體設計與製造行業的公司中排名領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 穎崴的整體曝險屬於低等水準，領先於半導體設計與製造行業的平均水準。公司所處之行業較
需要改善的 ESG 議題包含碳排放揭露、人力資本與供應鏈和產品治理等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：穎崴在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬中等。公司 ESG 資訊揭露較少，
並無對投資者和公眾的建立明確的問責機制。其 ESG 相關問題缺乏相關政策與措施，建議公司將這些整合到核心業務
戰略中。但公司並未涉及重大 ESG 爭議。 
 圖 20：ESG 分析

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

Update
Asia Vital Components Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
Takeaways from Asia AI Summit
2026
• AVC retains its 2026 outlook for sequential growth from both a revenue and
margin perspective. In 2027–28, AI ASIC projects will add incremental drivers
through shipment volume increases and high customization requirements.
Beyond 2028, China AI projects will see contribution growth as an additional
driver.
• AVC expects the new cold plate module project for the next-generation GPU

platform to begin volume shipments in 3Q26, while the new AI ASIC project
should start in late 3Q26 or early 4Q26.
• Two-phase liquid cooling represents a new thermal solution that AVC is
currently developing, targeting a 15% improvement in thermal performance.
Current tasks focus on pump design and air pressure management within
liquid loops.
• The capacity expansion plan this year aims to increase cold plate module
capacity by 5x. By end-2026, AVC targets a 50:50 capacity split between

China and Vietnam. The current plan for next year is to double the land area
at its Vietnam site.
• LEO satellite thermal solutions remain under development to meet high
reliability requirements across a wide operating temperature range.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 079: 260528_ms_GUC-DG

Source PDF: all report pdf/260528_ms_GUC-DG.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-05-28
Pages: 19
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

M
Idea
1) Tesla AI chip  market share gets diluted by Samsung foundry: GUC stock fell
10% today (vs. Taiex -2%). Investors are concerned about potential competition/
share loss for GUC's existing projects/customers. Given leading-edge capacity
shortages and higher TSMC wafer costs, our recent industry checks show that the
AI5 packaging configuration could shift from two AI5 chips to one AI5 plus a co-
packaged backup AI4 built on Samsung 5nm. What’s more, both Samsung and Intel

foundries are likely to produce next-gen 2nm AI6 as we noted in our previous
Foundry Floorplan
. Although we have not yet baked any AI6 revenue into our
current GUC forecast, we think it might disappoint some bullish investors who
expect GUC to handle the majority of the next-gen Tesla AI project.
2) Google CPU projects could be shared by Samsung foundry as well:  Google may
also seek other foundry suppliers or potentially other ASIC partners for more

capacity. According to our industry checks,
Samsung's foundry is also likely to
produce next-gen Google CPU (Axion 3) from its SF2 (or 2nm) process, with yields
gradually improving.  We see potential market share loss for GUC’s major CPU
business in the coming years, offsetting Google CPU demand strength at GUC. We
believe we have baked some conservatism into contribution from GUC’s major
projects — including Google CPU (2mn units in 2026 and 2.5mn in 2027 and Tesla

AI5 (800k units in 2027 and 1.5mn in 2028 — (without AI6). Therefore, we make no
changes to our earnings forecasts. We believe potential competition should have
limited near-term impact. However, we don't see upside to our current earnings
forecasts or price target of NT$4,888 either.
Move GUC to EW to take profits until we see new project wins:  With the medium-
/long-term outlook affected and after the share price rally over the past two
months (+135% vs. Taiex +33%), we think it’s time to take profits, with P/E now at

50x our 2027 EPS. We could turn constructive again if 1) TSMC can allocate more
wafers to GUC with price discounts to regain market share, and/or 2) GUC wins new
major CSP projects.
What’s Changed
Global Unichip Corp (3443.TW) From To
Rating Overweight Equal-weight
Global Unichip Corp  | Asia Pacific
Samsung foundry design service
competition creates market
share concern; downgrade to

Still strong chip demand from CPU  and AI business
How GUC  could participate in  TSMC's   automotive chip
foundry service
In TSMC: Thoughts about auto semi foundry dynamics; OW (1 Aug 2025), we noted that
the new AI5 will be manufactured using TSMC's 3nm process node and is expected to
deliver 4-5x higher performance than its predecessor, AI4. GUC could help in the design by
working closely with TSMC. We believe the Tesla AI project will only get bigger when the

new generation chip is introduced. For the  next-generation Tesla chip, despite the
competition from other foundries,  we still expect GUC to get involved in TSMC's version
of the AI chip too.
In the same report, we also noted that TSMC is the major autonomous driving chip
foundry for merchant chip suppliers such as Horizon Robotics and Nvidia (e.g., the Thor
chip).   We believe some of those merchant chips may go through a design service to ensure

time to market.
Some Chinese EV OEMs with custom ASIC chip designs are also potential customers for
Asian ASIC service providers and TSMC. For example, Alchip (OW) is starting the second
5nm ASIC for Li Auto at TSMC. XPeng is also seeking design service for its new generation
ASIC.
Exhibit 1: Tesla AI5
Source: Tesla
Strong Arm-based CPU revenue to dilute gross margin, but
still be positive for GUC's EPS

### Page 3

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 3
CPU turnkey revenue carries a low gross margin – likely in the single digits – because this
is a “Turnkey 2 or 3” project, where GUC provides advanced packaging design support (e.g.,
silicon interposers and ABF substrates) but does not own the photomasks for the ASIC
designs. Our checks  suggest that   Google CPU gross margin could also be 2-3ppts better
than our previous anticipation of 5% given the better order scale at TSMC. We now expect

strong Google CPU demand and already saw Axion CPU demand at least 7mn units from
2026-2028, generating close to US$5bn in revenue (ASP US$700) for GUC over the same
period.
GUC also helps with the Cobalt 200 CPU for Microsoft, an Arm-based design tailored for
Microsoft Cloud. Our checks also indicate strong demand for Cobalt at  >1mn units in 2027
and onwards. We therefore forecast US$500-600mn revenue contribution from this in
2027-2028.

Other major projects in 2026/2027
GUC has indicated that it will have three AI-related projects with US cloud service
providers (CSPs) active in 2026. We believe these CSP projects could account for over
50% of 2026e revenue and look for crypto-related momentum to continue at least

through 1H26, as the company has already secured foundry capacity support. In our view,
this underscores the importance of strong foundry relationships in obtaining scarce
leading-edge capacity. Including GUC’s other AI start-up engagements and IP licensing, we
estimate AI-related revenue will exceed 50% of total revenue in both 2026 and 2027.
• Microsoft: In our previous report, we indicated that GUC supports a Microsoft
3nm AI accelerator (Maia 200) TK2. Our industry checks suggest that Maia 200

tape-out should come in May 2026, with mass production later next year. GUC also
helps on the Cobalt 200 CPU, Arm-based design tailored for Microsoft Cloud.
• Amazon AI inference chips: GUC is helping AWS build the inference chip for AI
speakers, which could also start revenue contribution in 2026 and reach around
US$100mn in 2027.
• SanDisk and Kioxia SSD controllers:  From our industry checks, GUC has also been
working  with SanDisk on  SSD controllers, which should start to contribute some

NRE revenue in 2026, and then ~US$250mn in turnkey revenue in 2027.
• Chinese ADAS chipmaker:  In addition, GUC's auto customers are not limited to
Tesla only. A Chinese ADAS chipmaker  could also contribute revenue  by 2026 and
ramp in 2027.
• GLink IP licensing: In 2026, we also expect GUC to keep licensing its die-to-die
GLink IP to Google’s CPU project, which we estimate could generate US$3-5mn in
annualized revenue.

Price target remains unchanged at NT$4,888
Our price target  remains unchanged. All of our    key valuation assumptions are also
unchanged, including  cost of equity of 9.2% (a beta of 1.2, a risk premium of 6%, and a risk-
free rate of 2%), intermediate growth rate of 17%, and terminal growth rate of 5.0%.
Our bull and bear case values are also at NT$6,645  and NT$3,010,  respectively.

Relative valuation
GUC's share price has risen nearly 135% since April 2025 given the strong demand from
Google CPU and Tesla AI5 (the TAIEX is up 33% over the same period).
Our price target of NT$4,888 implies 48x our 2027 EPS estimate, lower than +1 SD from

GUC’s historical NTM P/E since 2013. We maintain our view that the design service market
will proliferate with AI ASIC outgrowing AI GPU in the long term. However, we think it’s
time  to take profits, with the stock now trading at 50x our 2027 EPS estimate with
potential competition.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
TSMC (2330.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of 1.2, risk-
free rate of 2.0% and risk premium of 6.0%), an intermediate growth rate of 10.5%, and a
terminal growth rate of 4.0%.
Risks to Upside
n TSMC charges large customers more to keep its gross margin above 56% in the long
term.
n AI semi demand grows more significantly than expected, while TSMC maintains high

market share in leading-edge foundry business.
n Outsourcing from Intel CPU increases in 2025-27.
Risks to Downside
n Inventory correction occurs in 2026.
n Demand for leading edge technologies weakens.
n Costs of overseas fabs grow significantly.
Alchip Technologies Ltd (3661.TW)
Base case value, derived from a residual income model. We assume the cost of equity is con-
stant at 10.4% (2% risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.4 beta). Our intermediate growth rate and

terminal growth rates are 16% and 5%, respectively.
Risks to Upside
n Demand for Chinese GPUs and AI chipsets takes off earlier than expected.
n NRE demand accelerates.
n Alchip wins more US hyperscaler HPC projects.
Risks to Downside
n China's HPC localization progress slows down.
n AI chipset progress is much slower to develop.
n NRE demand decelerates.
n Pricing competition intensifies.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Update
King Slide Works Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
Asia AI Summit 2026
Takeaways
• Rail kit demand: Management sees it coming from a variety of applications,
including server racks, switch racks, power racks, CPI, rack, storage racks, etc.
Management is confident to maintain the dominant share in the rail kit
market for both GPU and ASIC servers in the coming year, as it will stay as
the design partner for almost all new projects extending beyond 2029.

• GM trend
: It is likely to see modest expansions in 2026. Longer term, its
comprehensive patent portfolio and solid execution should help hold its GM
at an elevated level, since peers are less likely to catch up under the much
shorter design cycle.
•  Capacity expansion pipeline
: There is still plenty of capacity to support
growth in the near term, off the record high revenue of NT$2596mn (+35%
MoM/+79% YoY) in April, and more capacity expansions are in the pipeline.

US capacity will start mass production from September 2026 and customers
are willing to pay for the incremental costs, so there will be no impact to
King Slide’s margin.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
King Slide Works Co. Ltd. (2059.TW)
Base case, 34x 2027e P/E. We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology because we
believe it better captures King Slide's earnings growth potential. We believe our target mul-
tiple is justified by an operating profit CAGR of 30% seen for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 1.2.
This is in line with the average of 1.2 over 2010-24, when the stock was trading at 17-18x P/E

vs. an operating profit CAGR of 14%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected AI capex
n Stronger server demand
n Faster market share gains
Risks to Downside
n Softer AI capex
n Weaker server demand
n Sluggish kitchen furniture demand
n Competition leading to slower share gains.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

MediaTek  | Asia Pacific
Taiwan  AI Summit 2026 Key
Takeaways: From Cloud to Edge
MediaTek remains our Top Pick given its strong technology and
execution in Cloud AI ASIC design services. Edge AI also has
strong potential.
What’s new? MediaTek co-COO and CFO Mr. David Ku joined our inaugural AI
Summit to outline the company’s future AI semi strategy. Management views AI as
ubiquitous and plans to pursue a Cloud-to-Edge strategy to capture the opportunity.

We also joined a large-group IR meeting for near-term updates.
Cloud AI:
ASIC business development
• Management reiterated that MediaTek plans to keep adding customer value
and differentiating by technology to avoid ASIC commoditization under the
COT (customer-owned tooling) trend. MediaTek believes Cloud AI ASIC
operating margin can stay above the corporate average.
• Regarding “full system rack support” for Google TPU, management clarified
that MediaTek’s system integration is through chiplet and interconnect

technology, not rack systems or networking design.
• For agentic AI compute, MediaTek plans to participate in PC/server CPU
opportunities through merchant or ASIC design services. The prior NVIDIA
GB10 AI edge server is an example.
• The company doesn’t think major ASIC designs are limited to either training
or inference purposes. We therefore see volume upside for MediaTek’s TPU
v8 (ZebraFish) and v9 (HumuFish) vs. Street consensus which reflects

MediaTek ASIC being used only for AI training.
• After securing the 2nm ASIC (should be TPU v9), the company is bidding for
the next-generation project (which we think should be TPU v10 IceFish; see
our 25 May 2026 report, MediaTek: “Target Price Up” report
). We therefore
see multi-year growth through 2030 (IceFish revenue contribution in 2029
and 2030).
• MediaTek said it has received several CSP RFQs and hopes to add one more
CSP customer by end-2026 (besides Google).

Edge AI: Smartphone and automotive business
• The company said its future growth is “in addition” to smartphone, while
smartphone should continue to grow with edge compute. The company
doesn’t rule out AI service providers reaching out to MediaTek for future AI
phones, given MediaTek’s strong turnkey capability.
• Management sees growth potential in Automotive.
Stay OW, maintain Top Pick: This reflects the company's strong technology and
execution in Cloud AI ASIC design services. Edge AI also has strong potential.

### Page 2

Idea
2
Strong AI ASIC upside in 2028 from 2nm TPU
2nm TPU to adopt dual sourcing – both TSMC CoWoS‑L and Intel EMIB
TSMC’s CoWoS (Chip-on-Wafer-on-Substrate) is the industry’s leading 2.5D packaging
solution, particularly for AI and HPC applications. Its key advantage lies in the use of a
high-density interposer or RDL layer, which enables extremely wide, low-latency
connections between logic dies and HBM stacks. This makes it well suited for bandwidth-

intensive workloads such as GPUs and AI accelerators.
However, these benefits come at a cost: CoWoS – particularly the silicon interposer/bridge
– is expensive, capacity-constrained, and subject to yield challenges, including potential
warpage issues at larger chip sizes. Cost and manufacturing complexity remain key trade-
offs. As a result, we see TSMC’s CoWoS roadmap supporting only up to ~9.7x reticle size,
which does not appear sufficient for future AI or HPC chip designs.

We expect MediaTek to secure a portion of CoWoS capacity for 2nm TPU to ensure
minimum volume production, but we believe it will use Intel’s (covered by Joseph Moore)
EMIB-T as the primary solution to reduce costs and scale volume in 2028, if execution
proceeds well.
Exhibit 1: TSMC's roadmap for interposer size
Source: Winway, TSMC
Exhibit 2: Intel's EMIB can easily support larger chips  with
more reticles  (>12x) if its supply chain execution proceeds well

Source: Intel
Margin trend and full‑rack system support. In response to our question on providing
full-rack support during its earnings call (referencing recent Broadcom-Google
collaboration), MediaTek indicated it is in discussions with the customer. Management
expects any full‑rack system design support to materialize after 2027.
On margin trends, management highlighted continued value addition to the customer
across both silicon and packaging design. As a result, ASIC should remain accretive to

operating margins

### Page 3

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 3
Google 2nm TPU the major growth driver for MediaTek from
2028
On April 22, Google introduced TPU v8 at Google Cloud Next, featuring two purpose-built
architectures for training and inference: TPU 8t and TPU 8i. Our recent supply chain
checks across multiple companies on reticle size confirm that MediaTek’s 3nm TPU 8
corresponds to TPU 8t. This supports our view that MediaTek’s TPU performance should
be sufficient for AI training.

According to Google’s announcement, “both chips will be generally available later this
year and can be used as part of Google’s AI Hypercompute,” in line with MediaTek’s
guidance that AI ASIC will contribute ~US$2bn of revenue in 2026 (we now assume 450-
500k units at a US$4,500 ASP).
In addition, our recent checks suggest that ASP for the next-generation 2nm Humufish
TPU, supported by MediaTek, could exceed our prior expectations, driven by an increased

number of main dies. For Humufish, checks across multiple sources indicate the number of
main dies per chip could significantly exceed Zebrafish (one compute die with one I/O die).
Combined with a SerDes upgrade and larger packaging size, we estimate ASP at US$12-15k
(we now model US$13k).
At the gross margin level, management indicated margins vary by project, which may also
reflect potential full-rack system support. We model 2nm TPU gross margin at c.35% (vs.

c.40% for 3nm TPU) given larger revenue scale, while we still expect operating margin at
~20-25%, above the smartphone BU’s 15-20%.

### Page 5

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Android AI agent phones as an emerging trend from Google
I/O
Android AI agent phones – a trend to watch: Google is actively rolling out AI agents on
Android phones, starting with Galaxy S26 and Pixel 10 in early 2026. These agents can
perform multi-step tasks across apps – for example, ordering food, organizing notes, or
creating calendar events through direct interaction with app UIs. Over time, we expect
broader adoption across the Android ecosystem. Token consumption and cost remain key

constraints for agentic AI, and we believe on-device computing on edge devices such as
smartphones will help alleviate part of this cost burden.
Google I/O 2026 – Gemini evolves into an Agentic platform: At Google I/O 2026,
Google repositioned Gemini from a chatbot to a proactive AI “personal agent” capable of
autonomously handling tasks across apps and services. Google introduced several new
technologies, including Gemini Spark – a persistent assistant operating in the background

across Workspace tools – Gemini 3.5 Flash for faster real-time interaction, and Gemini
Omni for advanced multimodal capabilities spanning text, audio, images, and video. The
company also announced AI-driven changes to Google Search and deeper Gemini
integration across Android, YouTube, shopping, and future XR devices, signaling a broader
push to embed AI as a continuously available assistant that manages workflows and
automates user tasks. We believe the agentic AI replacement cycle could start in 2027

from high-end Android phones – another option value on MediaTek.
Exhibit 7: Gemini transforms into 'agentic' platform
Source: Google

Valuation Methodology and Risks
MediaTek (2454.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity 9.2%, intermediate growth
rate 12.0%, terminal growth rate 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Edge AI proliferates smartphone replacement cycle.
n Smartphone demand rises in China and other EMs.
n New products attract high demand, resulting in market share gains.
n Google's TPU spurs demand.
Risks to Downside
n Smartphone demand deteriorates in China and other EMs.

n Competition heats up, resulting in pricing competition.
n New products attract low demand, causing market share loss.
n Margin dilution is more severe.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Yageo Corp.  | Asia Pacific
Asia AI Summit 2026 Feedback
• Distributor inventory remains healthy at <5 months (vs. slightly above 5
months in 1Q).
• In early stages of silicon capacitor development. Currently seeking partners
• Book-to-bill ratio is now at 1.3x.
• Not seeing any signs of consumer electronics demand slowdown in 2Q, but
visibility into 2H is limited currently.
• AI revenue was of the mix 15% in C1Q. Outside of tantalum and resistors,

magnetics contribute more to AI than MLCC.
• Typically, pricing negotiations with customers are  quarterly,but Yageo did not
comment on whether it expects to hike MLCC prices in 2H.
• Yageo adjusted prices for tantalum three times in the past 1.5 years; each
time it was for a different tantalum product.

Key Takeaways
There weren't a lot of pricing adjustments baked into 1Q revenue.
Thus,  there should be more (from the pricing adjustments made in tantalum,
resistors, magnetics, MLCC, etc.).
2Q revenue and margin likely have upside –  demand appears stronger than a few
weeks ago...
...with standard (75%+) and premium products (85%+) all running at higher

utilization vs. its guidance during the 1Q earnings call.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Yageo Corp. (2327.TW)
Base-case, multistage residual income valuation model. Key assumptions: cost of equity 7.8%
(risk-free rate 1%, equity risk premium 6.8% and beta of 1.1), medium-term growth rate 10%,
terminal growth rate 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected revenue/profit recognition from stronger AI server/notebook
demand
n Further price hikes led by stronger pricing power arising from better demand and/or

tighter supply
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected end-demand recovery
n Escalating US/China trade tensions
n Weaker automotive and industrial demand

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

### Page 2

公司簡介：界霖成立於 2000 年，公司核心定位於功率導線架沖壓元件，擁有精密
模具設計、金屬精密沖壓、異型材製造及局部電鍍技術；產品廣泛應用於專用、工
控、汽車電子、AI 及邊緣運算（Edge）等 Power 元件領域目前營運據點包含：台
灣、日本、中國與馬來西亞。2025 年應用別營收比重：車用佔 49%、工業佔 29%
與消費性佔 22%。客戶包括：英飛凌、STM、on semi、瑞薩、Rohm，日月新集
團與強茂等等。 
受惠產品組合與銅價上漲， 1Q26 毛利率明顯增加 ：1Q26 營業 13.6 億元，
(+8%QoQ，+2%YoY)，受惠多項新產品自 4Q25 起陸續出貨，同時公司適時反映
銅價等原物料成本上漲並調整售價，1Q26 毛利率達 19%(4Q25 毛利率 15%)，營
業利益 1.34 億元，業外則因為匯率不利而有虧損 465.3 萬元，稅後 EPS 1.00 元。 
2026 年車用復甦：中國廠房受惠中國本土半導體實力提升，同步帶動導線架需求
增加；而馬來西亞廠區則受惠 IDM 廠持續擴產；英飛凌、On semi 與 STM 等國
際大廠皆於法說會透漏，車市歷經庫存修正後，訂單已明顯改善，預估同步帶動界
霖 2026 年車用應用將呈現成長。 
AI 相關展望樂觀：界霖以沖壓起家 ， 從早期單體導線架發展至到現在的Clip-Bond 
導線架，將沖壓與蝕刻整合，成功銷售給終端客戶，順利跨入 AI 產品領域，目前
AI 相關營收比重約佔 5~7%，隨著 AI SERVER 功率持續提升，HVDC 的導入，帶
動整產業的需求，歐美功率元件大廠亦皆表示，2026 年~2027 年 AI 相關的營收
展望皆呈現強勁的需求，預期界霖ＡＩ相關營收比重於 2027 年將達 10%以上。 
資本支出著重在製程優化：2026 年資本支出約在 2 億元出頭，前兩年資本支出約
落在 1 億元以上，雖然景氣稍有復甦，公司主要以整體優化角度進行開發，不會大
規模增添設備。 
財務預估 ：預估 2026 年營收 63.9 億元(+19%YoY)， 毛利率17.3%， 稅後EPS 4.63
元。預估 2027 年營收 67.4 億元(+5.5%YoY)，毛利率 16.9%，稅後 EPS 4.89 元。

預估 2Q26 營收季增 5%，推估 EPS 6.57 元 
1Q26 營收季增 7%、年增 18%，其中毛利率季增 0.3 個百分點達 13.6%，
OPM 季增 1.5 個百分點達 7.7%，本業獲利優於預期 16%，業外淨收益挹注
2.9 億元，EPS 6.88 元/季增 8%，優於元大預期 30%。展望 2Q26 延續先進
製程/封裝需求強勁，近期成熟製程稼動率亦接近滿載，使得半導體原材料需
求維持強勁，預估營收季增 5%、年增 15%，毛利率估計 持平，推估 EPS 
6.57 元/季減 4%、年增 38%，較前次報告上修 14%。 
先進製程、晶圓新廠擴張、工程大有斬獲，26 年營運展望佳 
1Q26 營收結構為光阻劑 50%、矽晶圓(佣金)1%、上海崇越 17%(矽晶圓)、
其他電子材料 18%、工程 8%及其他 6%，其中光阻劑、其他電子材料 YoY
成長>3 成，反映先進製程需求強勁 且隨製程推進有助內容價值 提升，2H26
受到上游石化原料上漲，預期售價將調漲 15~20%。矽晶圓來自先進製程續
強、成熟製程 UTR 上升而帶動量成長，然價格受限於 LTA，整體營收成長
持平至略增。工程部份，過往以電子/半導體廠轉往東南亞設廠等環工為主，
近期陸續台積、美光環境工程相關專案有所斬獲 ，目前在手訂單 >200 億元，
2026 年工程營收目標 YoY 成長 2 成，仍有上修空間，毛利率亦有機會提升。 
先進製程、自主供應鏈帶動中長期營運成長趨勢不變 
2026 年延續先進製程 帶動新材料開發及內容價值提升，搭配晶圓廠產能開
出及新增大型工程專案，加上 2H26 原材料醞釀漲價，營運展望更佳，其中
預估光阻、半導體材料全年營收成長 >2 成，上海崇越/矽晶圓持平至略增、
工程成長 2 成，公司營收目標雙位數成長。27 年隨大客戶新製程/新產能大
量開出，展望更佳。本中心預估 2026 / 202 7 年 EPS 27.91/30.83 元，分別
上修 17%/24%，係因先進製程原材料需求優於預期，加上漲價效益，工程
在手訂單亦較年初倍增等，上修營收假設基礎，毛利率亦有機會略微提升 。

元大觀點 
⧫  
展望 2Q26 原材料需求維持高檔，預估營收季增 5%，推估 EPS6.57
元，季減 4%、年增 38%。 
⧫  
2026 年來自先進製程需求強勁、晶圓廠稼動率提升、原材料漲價效
應，加上工程在手訂單創新高，全年營收成長有機會>2 成。 
⧫  
最新預估 2026/27 年 EPS 27.91 /30.83 元，調幅 17%/23%。維持買
進，評價基礎改以 15 倍本益比、2027 年 EPS，基於主力客戶先進製
程、海外擴廠效益推升中長期成長趨勢明確，帶動評價 面有所提升。

營運分析  
預估 2Q26 營收季增 5%，推估 EPS 6.57 元 
1Q26 營收季增 7%、年增 18%，其中毛利率季增 0.2 個百分點達 13.6%，OPM 季增 1.4 個百分點
達 7.7%，本業獲利優於預期 16%，業外淨收益挹注 2.9 億元，EPS 6.88 元/季增 8%，優於元大預
期 30%。展望 2Q26 延續先進製程/封裝需求強勁，近期成熟製程稼動率亦接近滿載，使得半導體
原材料需求維持強勁，預估營收季增 5%、年增 15%，毛利率估計持平，推估 EPS 6.57 元/季減
4%、年增 38%，較前次報告上修 14%。

### Page 3

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體設備與耗材 2026 年 5 月 22 日 Topco 第3 頁，共 13頁

先進製程、晶圓新廠擴張、工程大有斬獲，26 年營運展望佳 
1Q26 營收結構為光阻劑 50%、矽晶圓(佣金)1%、上海崇越 17%(矽晶圓)、其他電子材料 18%、工
程 8%及其他 6%，其中光阻劑、其他電子材料 YoY 成長>3 成，反映先進製程需求強勁且隨製程推
進有助內容價值提升，2H26 受到上游石化原料上漲，預期售價將調漲 15~20%。矽晶圓來自先進
製程續強、成熟製程 UTR 上升而帶動量成長，然價格受限於 LTA，整體營收成長持平至略增。工
程部份，過往以電子/半導體廠轉往東南亞設廠 等環工為主 ，近期陸續台積、美光環境工程相關專
案有所斬獲，目前在手訂單>200 億元，2026 年工程營收目標 YoY 成長 2 成，仍有上修空間，毛
利率亦有機會提升。

先進製程、自主供應鏈帶動中長期營運成長趨勢不變 
2026 年延續先進製程帶動新材料開發及內容價值提升，搭配晶圓廠產能開出 及新增大型工程專案，
加上 2H26 原材料醞釀漲價，營運展望更佳，其中預估光阻、半導體材料全年營收成長>2 成，上海
崇越/矽晶圓持平至略增、工程成長 2 成，公司營收目標雙位數成長。27 年隨大客戶新製程/新產能
大量開出，展望更佳。本中心預估 2026 / 2027 年 EPS27.91/30.83 元，分別上修 17%/24%，係因
先進製程原材料需求優於預期，加上漲價效益，工程在手訂單亦較年初倍增等，上修營收假設基礎，
毛利率亦有機會略微提升。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體設備與耗材 2026 年 5 月 22 日 Topco 第4 頁，共 13頁  
產業概況  
崇越科技主要代理日本信越石英株式會社 (Shin-Etsu Chemical) 銷售半導體、光電、電子材料各類
產品，其中以矽晶圓、光阻劑佔營收比重最高，分別佔營收約 4 成、3 成，以下針對兩產業概況分
別說明：

庫存改善，新應用、新產能帶動矽晶圓景氣復甦明確 
過去 2 年矽晶圓產業反映庫存調整、總體經濟/關稅不確定性、消費性需求不振、加上高 LTA 造成
存貨堆積，使得這一波矽晶圓景氣復甦遞延及循環週期拉長。 2023~24 年出貨皆呈現衰退走勢，
2025 年出貨面積成長 5%，仍低於 SEMI 原預估 10%，主因除了 AI 強勁，non AI 包括消費性、工
控、車用等復甦力道仍疲弱。展望 2026 年來自 AI/HPC/先進製程/先進封裝/HBM 等需求強勁，加
上晶圓廠新產能開出，加速庫存調整，根據 SEMI 預估 2026、27 年矽晶圓出貨面積成長 5%、9%，
產業復甦方向不變。 
2026 年供過於求收斂，先進製程/記憶體應用的矽晶圓醞釀漲價 
矽晶圓產業隨著半導體景氣 、市場供需狀況而波動 ，預估 2025/26/27 年矽晶圓供給分別年增
5%/4%/4%。本中心 推估 2026 年整體矽晶圓需求年增 10%，預估 2025/26/27 年供過於求比
119%/112%/104%，預估 2026 年供過於求比逐步收斂， 27 年接近平衡。價格走勢，儘管行業別
LTA 比例高，但受到終端需求疲弱及庫存調整速度緩慢，25 年部份廠商採 LTA 搭配現貨方式銷售 ，
使得 blended ASP 呈下滑走勢。26 年隨 AI/先進製程/記憶體需求強勁，相關應用 Wafer 醞釀漲價
或提高 LTA 比例帶動 blended ASP 有所提升，然矽晶圓產業全面反轉向上有機會落在 2027 年。

EUV 光阻劑持續供不應求，KrF、ArF 光阻劑受稼動率下滑影響需求 
光阻劑(Photoresist)是光敏感材料，經曝光顯影後可幫助晶圓製程完成電路設計的圖案轉移。而依
曝光的光源不同，所需要的光阻劑類型亦有所差異，應用於紫外線(UV)的為 g-line 和 i-line 光阻劑，
應用於深紫外線(DUV)的為 KrF 與 ArF 光阻劑，而應用於超紫外線(EUV)的光阻劑則為目前最新技
術之 EUV 光阻劑。根據調研機構預估，隨半導體需求持續擴張，預期全球光阻劑市場將在未來 5-
10 年持續以 5.5%的年複合成長率成長。目前全球供應光阻劑的廠商以日商為主，包括信越化學、
東京應用化學和 JSR，崇越主要代理日本信越化學的光阻劑產品，根據公司表示目前先進製程
EUV 光阻劑仍處於供不應求的狀態，而應用於深紫外線(DUV)的 KrF、ArF 光阻劑，則因反映晶圓
廠稼動率而需求略顯疲弱。

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體設備與耗材 2026 年 5 月 22 日 Topco 第6 頁，共 13頁  
獲利調整與股票評價  
維持買進、目標價調升至 480 元 
本中心預估崇越 2026/2027 年 EPS 分別為 27.91 /30.83 元，分別上修 17%/24%，主要調整包括原
材料成長包括光阻、晶圓載具等及新增先進封裝應用的需求優於預期，並且 2H26 將調漲售價 15-
20%，另外，工程在手訂單由前次 100 億元提升至 200 億元，因而調高 26/27 年營收成長預估分
別由 16%/6%上修至 20%/12%，毛利率受產品組合轉佳而略微調高 0.3~0.5 個百分點。 
展望 2026 年，AI/HPC/車用新興應用帶動半導體新材料的開發及內容價值提升，搭配新晶圓廠產
能開出等推升營運動能更為強勁、海外工程估計成長 2 成。27 年延續大客戶新晶圓產能、新製程
陸續開出，推升營運續創新高。根據 2026 / 202 7 年預估獲利換算本益比分別為 15/14 倍，目前本
益比落於歷史區間 8-15 倍之中間偏上緣，約相當於國內同業平均本益比 16/15 倍，反映市場看好
中長期先進製程、主力客戶海外擴廠效益，亦使 訂單能見度高，相關供應鏈評價亦有所提升，加上
股息配發率達 6-7 成，兼具現金殖利率概念。維持買進評等， 評價基礎改以 15 倍目標本益比、
2027 年 EPS 30.83 元，推得目標價 480 元。

### Page 10

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體設備與耗材 2026 年 5 月 22 日 Topco 第10 頁，共 13頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 崇越整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於中風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排行於略微領先的位置，
但在半導體設備行業中排名略微領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 崇越的整體曝險屬於低等水準，但略低於半導體設備行業的平均水準。公司較需要改善的  ESG 
議題包含人力資本、商業道德及資源使用等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：崇越在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬中等。崇越於 ESG 相關揭露程度
高，顯示其對投資人及大眾的責任感極佳，雖說公司有針對重大 ESG 議題進行管控，但對於關鍵領域仍缺乏相關政策
及計畫。然而，目前崇越尚未涉及任何 ESG 重大爭議。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

註：元大給予個股之目標價係依 12 個月投資期間計算。大中華探索系列報告並無正式之 12 個月目標價，其投資建議乃根據分析師報告中之指定期間分析
而得。

1 
6285 啟碁 
(Wistron NeWeb Co.)

報告日期： 2026/05/22 研 究 員：方姿婷  (Lesley Fang) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 啟碁為通訊設備網通代工廠，持續調整產品佈局  
啟碁(6285)為網通大廠主要提供通訊設備 ODM 服務，具備天線設
計、軟硬體整合、機構設計、系統整合、介面開發與產品測試等
服務，開發各頻段無線通訊設備，從衛星通訊設備、衛星廣播、
衛星電視等，切入行動通訊、車用工控、智慧家庭、物聯網等產
品線。啟碁依照產品別應用分為，S e r v ic e  P rov id e r 佔 41 % 、
Automotive 與 Industrial 佔 27%、Enterprise 佔 31%、Others 佔 1%。
啟碁產能佔比在台灣佔 35%、越南佔 40%、中國佔 15%，越南二
期約佔 5%、墨西哥廠約佔 5%，預期 2026 年越南廠佔比提升至
50% 。 銷售區域別美洲佔 72% 、 歐洲佔 12% 、 亞洲 16% 。 啟碁認為，
北美與歐洲市場 5G FWA、企業網通設備、低軌衛星設備拉貨增
加、及車用 ADAS 也照計畫進行中，整體而言，2026 年啟碁隨著
網路交換器(高階 Core Switch)進入 800G Switch 產業上升循環，啟
碁預期將有 AI Server 用之高階網路交換器(800G Switch 新品)貢獻
營運，2026 年起 800G Switch 放量佔 2026 年營收達 5%(約當 50~60
億元)，預期 800G Switch 2027 年後營收可望達成 10~15%營收佔
比，啟碁切入 800G Switch 時程較晚以品牌端、企業端用戶為主，
啟碁會開始切入 Data Center 以及 Edge Switch ， 與母公司緯創(3231)
經營市場有所區隔但也部分重疊。展望 2026 年， 啟碁於 OCS(光學
網路交換器)佈局有成。啟碁始導入 OCS(光學網路交換器)，預期
27 年 OCS 會貢獻營收，啟碁 OCS 技術層次類似 Cohr 技術，但應
用層面，因為 Switch 客戶在 AI 架構下面臨高耗能的需求，OCS(光
學網路交換器)導入之後足以降低能耗達 90~95% 。 啟碁採用關鍵的
WSS(Liquid Crystal on Silicon MEMS 結構去做)智慧型光通訊交換
技術，動態管理波長、波長選擇開關，與 MEMS 技術去做調變解
調訊號。

啟碁車用佈局(車用細分為車載資通訊佔大宗、衛星廣播、ADAS、
模組與天線)，汽車電子市場的屬性深耕產品與服務，提供模組感
應器 ADAS 等產品，直接提供服務給電動車車廠客戶，車用部分
也開始出貨北美的車用充電樁(模組、整機)都有出貨，預期車用充
電樁含整機、模組，相當於絕對營收金額 50~100 億元。此外，北
美衛星廣播營運商 Sirius-XM 之車用衛星廣播模組，為啟碁車用耕
投資建議：買進

耘多年之客戶之一，可望受惠於 SiriusXM 在 2025 年 CES 展場中
指出未來車用衛星廣播、串流和播客領域的最新技術發展，分享
數位轉型中的創新成果，宏遠投顧預期，車用客戶的創新開發與
進程，將促使啟碁在車用市場持續保有其車用無線通訊模組、車
用衛星廣播模組 、 車聯網與車用通訊模組等供貨實力與營運動能。

二. 長期企業需求、5G FWA 放量，啟碁仍為受惠廠商 
啟碁過去耕耘天線設計多年、材料選擇、封裝、測試至模組化能
力 ， 微型裝置天線模組化量產能力佳 ， 可望 AiP(Antenna-in-Package)
集成封裝模組競爭中脫穎而出。因客戶生產多元與風散生產基地
考量，啟碁越南產能加入營運，8.3 萬平方米的廠房空間，建置越
南二期廠房，2025~2026 年提升越 南廠的產能。展望 2026 年，啟
碁認為歐洲 5G FWA 量產、美國地區企業網通市佔率有所提升，
受惠北美寬頻基建法案挹注，啟碁於美國也有生產據點，5G FWA
會於 2025 年開始放量且區域來說會是在北美、歐洲為主。2026 年
啟碁北美佔啟碁營收達 67%(未來會將北美地區營收佔比調整至合
適的比重) ， 成長多數是在北美地區 ， 產品線包含 Connected Home、
車用、網通、企業端、低軌、WiFi-7 等產品。啟碁歐洲市場營收
約 12~14%，因歐洲經濟未見起色拉貨偏弱，但有看到其他中國網
通廠商，在歐洲市場供貨遭遇難題，而有看到轉單給台廠的效應。
啟碁提及 5G CPE(包含 Gateway、 Module 、FWA 、Router、光纖
等 )非單指 5G FWA 佔啟碁營收比重 10% ， 受惠於美國基建對於光
纖 CABLE、FWA 都有感受到拉貨，因應 5G FWA 在歐洲市場擴展
有成，2026 年 5G FWA 也會隨之增長。

三.  低軌道衛星對啟碁具正向貢獻，預期毛利率優化 
根據低軌衛星的領先廠商：SpaceX、Telesat、Oneweb、Amazon 均
為台灣網通廠商的客戶或潛在客戶，據供應鏈訪查得知，在低軌
衛星客戶(S 客戶進入產品汰換期 FCC 核准發射總量為第 2 代 
3 萬顆(評估初期會先有 4,000~5,000 顆) 、O 客戶也追加數量至上
千顆、美系 A 客戶期望在 2 年先發射規劃的一半數量)。因此，
宏遠投顧認為 2024~2026 年之間，低軌衛星的總量會從市場原先
預期 1.5~2 萬顆，上修至 3 萬顆+，天上衛星量增對應之地面接
收站量體也會同步增加(評估至少有數百萬座屋頂型地面接收
站) 。 宏遠投顧估計此低軌道衛星之地面接收站商機 ， 對於啟碁 2024
年營收貢獻介於 5~10%之間，在近期低軌衛星量增之下營收比重
接近 10% ，低軌衛星營運比重於 2025~2027 年持續增加中，低軌道
衛星延續 2~3 年進入成長爆發期，對於啟碁營運帶來正向貢獻。 
宏遠投顧認為，低軌衛星發射量可望隨成本下降而提升，衛星地
面接收站體之相位是陣列天線設計有其難度，通訊用天線設計為

波長的 1/4~1/2，隨著傳播頻率愈高，天線長度愈短，在 5G 時代
mmWave 天線集成高頻率通訊、MIMO 載波聚合多天線技術等，對
天線、射頻前端設計、多工器件、濾波元件用量產生關鍵影響，
而啟碁過去的強項在於天線設計，低軌衛星地面接收站體天線強
調，波束、快速追蹤天上衛星、快速轉向等難度，需要進行多次
研發、測試也墊高啟碁的競爭優勢。低軌(LEO)衛星專案，啟碁指
出，低軌訂單能見度在客戶改款之後一季比一季能見度高，部分
客戶有開始追加訂單的情況。展望 2026 年，因應產業供應鏈確認
啟碁此美系客戶 LEO 量增加至少+30~50%YoY。啟碁有整合 LEO
之地面接收站體用之多波束整合型天線(Mu lti Beam Antenna)供應
給其 LEO 客戶所用，預期 LEO 產品線量能增長，對於公司毛利率
有正向挹注。 再者， 啟碁為 S 客戶地面接收站模組之唯一供應商，
訂閱用戶數增長會帶動地面接收站量體增長，預計 2026 年低軌衛
星營運成長+40~50%YoY，依此評估，低軌衛星營收佔 2026 年啟
碁營收可望增長至 15~20%之間。

四.投資建議：買進，目標價 325 元 
啟碁科技競爭優勢為天線與 RF 設計能力，專精於寬頻、多媒體、
物聯網通訊領域等應用，也具有無線領域之通訊技術，微波通訊、
數位無線通訊、天線設計、軟體工程、多媒體整合等經驗，低軌
道衛星商機，亦為啟碁一大營運成長機會。展望 2026 年，預估啟
碁年營收為 1351.42 億元，+22.58%YoY，毛利率為 13.66%，稅後
淨利為 59.41 億元，稅後 EPPS+12.28 元；展望 2027 年，預估啟碁
年營收 1537.32 億元，+13.76%YoY，毛利率為 13.60%，稅後淨利
為 69.27 億元 ， 稅後 EPS+14.32 元 。 啟碁企業用 WiFi-6/WiFi-6e(WiFi-7
滲透率於 2024 年將超越 10%，預期 2025 年網通 WiFi-7 佔啟碁營
收比重來到 10~15%，2026 年在企業端 WiFi-7 滲透率將超越 50%) 
AP、企業換機之高階網路交換器(800G Switch)，5G FWA 成為新舊
產品轉換之成長動能。基於網通產業適逢調整期，給予啟碁投資
建議維持買進，目標價 325 元(23.00 x2027 EPS)。

貳、公 司 概 況

一.公司簡介 
啟碁(6285)為緯創集團旗下子公司，緯創持股啟碁比例約 21.5%，
主要提供通訊設備 ODM 服務，具備天線設計、軟硬體整合、機構
設計、系統整合、介面開發與產品測試等服務，開發各頻段無線
通訊設備，從衛星通訊設備、衛星廣播、衛星電視等，切入行動
通訊、車用工控、智慧家庭、物聯網等產品線。
